TL;DR: 本文介绍了ANDES望远镜高分辨率光谱仪中冠状仪（CORO）模块的模拟性能，旨在支持系外行星大气表征，结合高对比度成像与高色散光谱（R=100,000），并利用APU工具评估探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足ELT上ANDES仪器对系外行星大气表征的科学需求，需发展具备高对比度成像与高分辨率光谱能力的冠状仪模块。

Method: 基于SCAO残差误差建模，开展CORO模块设计及性能仿真，并使用ANDES性能展开（APU）模拟工具评估其在发射光与反射光中探测系外行星的能力。

Result: 获得了CORO模块在不同条件下的初步对比度性能与系外行星探测预期结果，验证了其在高空间与角分辨率下探测大气原子/分子特征的可行性。

Conclusion: ANDES CORO模块有望在2035年首光后，实现系外行星大气的高精度联合成像与光谱表征，显著提升ELT在该领域的科学产出能力。

Abstract: We present the simulated performance of the coronagraph (CORO) module for ANDES, the Extremely Large Telescope (ELT) high-resolution spectrograph. ANDES aims to address a broad range of science cases, including the characterization of the atmosphere of exoplanets. With a first light envisioned by 2035, the instrument baseline features a modular fiber-fed echelle spectrograph with visible and near-infrared ultra-stable spectral arms to provide a simultaneous spectral range of 0.4-1.8\,$μ$m with a spectral resolving power up to 100,000. ANDES also includes an Integral Field Unit (IFU) mode-fed by a single-conjugate adaptive optics (SCAO) module and an insertable CORO module, enabling the combination of high-contrast imaging and high-dispersion spectroscopy (R=100,000) for the study of exoplanet properties. In this contribution, the ANDES CORO design, its main features and its simulated performance are detailed in the presence of SCAO residual errors to probe exoplanet atmospheres with the spectro-imager mode at high-spatial resolution and with unprecedented angular resolution. We show the preliminary simulation results on the predicted contrast and the expected detection performance with ANDES performance unfolded (APU), the simulation tool to determine the yield of exoplanets that will be detected in emitted and possibly reflected light. The characterization operation of the instrument will be discussed to assess the ANDES ability to detect atomic and molecular signatures connected to the exoplanet atmosphere characteristics.

</details>


### [114] [Breakthrough Listen's Automated Commensal Technosignature Survey with MeerKAT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23651)
*Daniel J. Czech,David H. E. MacMahon,Ian Heywood,Chenoa D. Tremblay,Matt Lebofsky,Kevin Lacker,Cherry Ng,Dave Horn,Sarah Buchner,Brian Lacki,Alex Andersson,Joe S. Bright,Steve Croft,Dave R. DeBoer,Jamie Drew,Vishal Gajjar,Peter Ma,Alex W. Pollak,Danny Price,Mark Ruzindana,Andrew P. V. Siemion,S. Pete Worden,Fernando Camilo*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Breakthrough Listen用户自供设备（BLUSE）系统在南非MeerKAT射电望远镜上的应用，该系统利用组播以太网实现自主、共时的宽频带地外技术信号搜寻，已处理超120万个观测点数据，验证了共时SETI巡天的高效性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SETI需要大量望远镜时间，而现代射电望远镜支持共时观测；为提升技术信号搜寻效率并兼顾其他科学目标，需构建高效、低成本的共时SETI系统。

Method: 基于MeerKAT的组播以太网接口，设计BLUSE系统：对F-engine原始数据进行上信道化至~1Hz分辨率，合成目标源的相干波束，并实时搜索技术信号；全程自主运行。

Result: 自2022年起，BLUSE已自主处理超120万个点源的相干波束数据（含重复观测）；实验证明其架构可靠、性能达标，并显著提升技术信号巡天速率。

Conclusion: BLUSE成功验证了在大型射电阵列上开展共时、宽频、自主SETI巡天的技术可行性与高性价比，为未来大规模技术信号搜寻提供了可扩展范式。

Abstract: The search for extraterrestrial intelligence (SETI) is an ongoing effort to detect technosignatures, evidence of technologically capable life beyond Earth. Conducting a comprehensive SETI programme requires a large amount of telescope time, which must be balanced with the science goals of a given observatory. Fortunately, many modern radio telescopes offer commensal access to the data they produce, allowing multiple scientific programmes to operate in parallel. The MeerKAT radio telescope in South Africa provides commensal access to a range of components, from each antenna's digitiser to the main channeliser (F-engine), via multicast Ethernet groups. Here, we describe the Breakthrough Listen user-supplied equipment (BLUSE) system at MeerKAT, which leverages multicast Ethernet to conduct an autonomous commensal technosignature survey, processing the full available bandwidth from all antennas. Its primary mode of operation is to upchannelise the incoming F-engine data to ~1Hz resolution, synthesize coherent beams on objects of interest, and search the resultant data for technosignatures. Since 2022, BLUSE has autonomously processed data from coherent beams synthesized on more than 1.2 million individual pointings, including repeat visits. BLUSE demonstrates how commensal technosignature surveys on radio telescope arrays offer a rapid and cost-effective way to increase the rate at which technosignature surveys can be conducted. This article describes the architecture of BLUSE, provides experimental evidence validating its features and performance, and quantifies its observing progress over the past few years. We also discuss the technical evolution of BLUSE, examine challenges faced and addressed, and consider avenues for future research and development.

</details>


### [115] [Progress of the design of the 12-m optical, Wide-field Spectroscopic Telescope (WST)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23786)
*P. Dierickx,G. Gausachs,C. Cudennec,D. Lee,R. Bacon,T. Travouillon,O. Bellido-Tirado,B. Sassolas,L. Pinard,S. DÁuria,C. Del Vecchio,V. De Caprio,V. Cianniello,W. Sutherland,P. Doel,T. Augusteijn,R. Navarro,L. Fréour,W. Saunders*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: WST是一个12米级宽视场光谱望远镜，具备同时进行多目标光谱（MOS）和积分场光谱（IFS）观测的能力，采用改进型卡塞格林光学设计，依托VLT/ELT成熟技术以降低风险，并注重可持续性设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足宽视场、高效率光谱观测需求，同时兼顾技术可行性、运行可靠性与可持续性，推动下一代地面光学望远镜发展。

Method: 采用修正卡塞格林光学系统，通过光纤将光分别导至MOS和IFS焦面；IFS焦面置于重力稳定积分场站内；光学设计借鉴VLT/ELT成熟方案，并集成自然导星地层自适应光学校正能力。

Result: 已完成Phase A立项与初步设计，关键子系统功能与物理定义进展顺利，高性能光学镀膜设计取得进展，选址确定为ESO帕纳尔-阿玛宗斯台址，整体设计兼顾性能、风险控制与可持续性。

Conclusion: WST项目在科学能力、工程可行性与可持续性之间取得良好平衡，是未来十年极具潜力的中等口径前沿光谱巡天设施。

Abstract: The Wide-Field Spectroscopic Telescope (WST) is a 12-m class, Alt-Az optical telescope providing two simultaneous foci, 2 degrees and 3 by 3 arc minutes respectively, for Multi-Object Spectroscopy (MOS) and Integral Field Spectroscopy (IFS) at visible wavelengths. Within the framework of the Horizon Programme, the project has been approved for phase A funding by the EC in 2024 and is currently undergoing intensive development. The optical design relies on a corrected Cassegrain solution feeding multi-object spectrographs through fibres, while the central area of the field is propagated to a gravity-stable Integral Field Station housing an array of spectrographs. We report progress of the design, of the functional and physical definitions of key subsystems, and of the design of high-performance coatings of the telescope optics. The telescope would be located near ESO Paranal - Armazones observatory and protected by a VLT-like enclosure. With a view to minimising risks, from capital investment to operations, designs build extensively on VLT and ELT solutions. Owing to the optical configuration, the telescope can be wavefront-controlled at both foci, with little if any crosstalk between MOS and IFS controls. The optical path is constrained to provide natural guide star, ground-layer adaptive correction at the IFS field, within existing technology. Finally, from its earliest stage of development the system is being scrutinized for its properties in relation to sustainability.

</details>


### [116] [HELIOS: An LLM-Driven Autonomous Indirect Trajectory Optimization Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24051)
*An-yi Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出HELIOS系统，利用大语言模型自动完成低推力星际轨迹优化中的庞特里亚金最小值原理（PMP）符号推导、验证、C++打靶代码生成与数值求解，克服传统间接法中约束适配难、动力学模型耦合紧、初值敏感三大瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于PMP的间接法在低推力轨迹优化中存在三类实践瓶颈：需逐例推导横截条件、不同动力学模型需重复编码、打靶方程对初值高度敏感。

Method: 构建基于大语言模型的HELIOS代理系统，支持自然语言输入，自动完成PMP符号推导、SymPy验证、C++打靶代码生成及数值求解；提出约束自适应推导框架、动力学自适应四模块代码生成机制、覆盖关键易错点的通用PMP推导规则集。

Result: 在11个递进式测试场景（含多段驻留转移、引力弹弓、太阳帆最小时间转移等）中，编译成功率100%（11/11）；8种开源LLM后端对比显示模型规模与推导能力呈正相关。

Conclusion: HELIOS实现了PMP推导与求解全流程自动化，验证了LLM在航天轨道优化高精度符号推理任务中的可行性与可扩展性，为智能航天任务设计提供新范式。

Abstract: Low-thrust trajectory optimization is a core technology in deep-space mission design. Indirect methods based on Pontryagin's Minimum Principle (PMP) offer rigorous optimality guarantees, yet their practical application faces three bottlenecks: (1) transversality conditions must be derived case by case for each constraint type; (2) different dynamics models require repeated code rewrites; and (3) shooting equations are highly sensitive to initial guesses. This paper presents HELIOS (Heuristic Engine for Low-thrust Interplanetary Optimization System), a trajectory optimization agent built around a large language model (LLM). Given a physical problem described in natural language, the system autonomously performs PMP symbolic derivation, SymPy verification, C++ shooting-code generation, and numerical solution without human intervention. Key innovations include: (1) a constraint-adaptive derivation framework that unifies arbitrary constraints into psi(x,p)=0 form and automatically generates stationarity conditions for free parameters (e.g., gravity-assist turning angle); (2) dynamics-adaptive four-module code generation supporting non-standard dynamics (solar sail, J2 perturbation) without modifying the underlying template; and (3) a general derivation rule set covering critical error-prone points in PMP derivation. Experiments on 11 progressive test scenarios show that HELIOS correctly derives and solves problems from simple rendezvous (8 variables) to multi-leg stay transfers (48 variables), gravity-assist trajectories (17 variables), and solar-sail minimum-time transfers (8 variables). The best compilation success rate reaches 100% (11/11). A multi-model comparison (8 open-source LLM backends, total scores 250-905) verifies the model-agnostic architecture and reveals a positive correlation between model scale and derivation capability.

</details>


### [117] [First high-contrast results on THD2 testbed after infrastructure upgrade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24229)
*Iva Laginja,Pierre Baudoz,Rémi Soummer,Emiel H. Por,Axel Potier,Johan Mazoyer,Raphaël Galicher,David Doelman,Rico Landman,Arnaud Sevin,Erin Pougheon,Corentin Paviot,Frans Snik,Felix Bettonvil,Jeroen Rietjens,Chris van Dijk,Kristien Peeters,Alexander Eigenraam,Mariya Krasteva,Matteo Taccola*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了升级后的THD2高对比度成像测试平台的首批科学成果，包括为Roman空间望远镜优化的高斯型多样性探针和ESA SUPPPPRESS项目中新型偏振无关矢量涡旋日冕仪的设计与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升高对比度成像能力，支持Roman空间望远镜早期观测及ESA SUPPPPRESS项目下一代日冕仪技术发展。

Method: 利用升级后的THD2测试平台，开展高斯型与sinc型多样性探针对比实验，并测试新型偏振无关矢量涡旋日冕仪在窄带与宽带光下的性能，结合主动焦面波前控制。

Result: 高斯型探针优于sinc型，提升电场估计效率；新型矢量涡旋日冕仪实现接近1e-10的高对比度（20%带宽）；THD2平台达到1e-8至1e-9对比度。

Conclusion: THD2平台显著增强欧洲在高对比度成像领域的技术能力，为未来空间任务提供关键技术支持。

Abstract: We present the first scientific results delivered by the upgraded THD2 high-contrast imaging testbed. We report two advances enabled by its improved stability and broadband performance. First, for the Roman Space Telescope, we demonstrate that Gaussian-shaped diversity probes outperform the baseline sinc probes by reducing non-linearities, supporting higher probe amplitudes, and improving electric field estimation efficiency. These results have led to their prioritization as an enhanced early observation for Roman. Second, within ESA's SUPPPPRESS project, we test new polarization-independent Vector Vortex Coronagraphs and design them to high-contrast performance approaching 1e-10 over a 20% bandwidth. We assess their behavior in narrow- and broadband light with active focal-plane wavefront control. Together, these results show how THD2 strengthens Europe's capability in high-contrast imaging, providing a unique platform reaching contrasts of 1e-8 to 1e-9 for developing next-generation coronagraphic technologies.

</details>


### [118] [Sensitivity of Laguerre-Gaussian Modes to Misalignment and Mode Mismatch in Gravitational-Wave Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24366)
*Liu Tao,Matteo Barsuglia*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文分析了高阶拉盖尔-高斯（LG）模式在引力波探测器中的耦合损耗，发现LG_{0,ℓ}模式对模式失配具有更强鲁棒性，更适合精密光学应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高引力波探测器中高阶LG模式的腔内耦合效率，降低热噪声，提升探测灵敏度。

Method: 通过解析和数值方法评估了通用LG_{p,ℓ}光束因横向/角向失调及束腰尺寸/位置失配导致的耦合损耗。

Result: 角向或横向失调引起的主导阶损耗随2p+|ℓ|+1增长；束腰失配损耗随2p²+2p+(2p+1)|ℓ|+1增长；而LG_{0,ℓ}模式的失配损耗仅线性依赖于|ℓ|+1。

Conclusion: LG_{0,ℓ}模式因其对模式失配的鲁棒性、宽平强度分布及中心暗区特性，在精密干涉仪（如引力波探测器）中更具实用优势。

Abstract: Higher-order Laguerre-Gaussian (LG) modes have broader and more uniform transverse intensity distributions than the fundamental Gaussian mode, making them attractive for precision optical applications. In gravitational-wave detectors, their enhanced spatial averaging of thermally driven test-mass fluctuations can reduce thermal noise. Their implementation, however, requires efficient coupling of the injected beam to the target cavity eigenmode. Residual misalignment and mode mismatch couple power out of the desired mode, reducing intracavity power buildup and degrading detector sensitivity. Here, we analytically and numerically evaluate the coupling loss induced by misalignment and mode mismatch for a generic $\mathrm{LG}_{p,\ell}$ beam. We show that the leading-order loss due to angular or lateral misalignment scales as $2p+|\ell|+1$, whereas that due to waist size or waist position mismatch scales as $2p^2+2p+(2p+1)|\ell|+1$. Although sensitivity to imperfect coupling generally increases with mode order, the donut-shaped $\mathrm{LG}_{0,\ell}$ family exhibits favorable mode mismatch scaling: its loss factor reduces to $|\ell|+1$ and therefore increases only linearly with the azimuthal index. This robustness, together with their broad intensity profiles and central dark regions that permit selective mirror masking, provides additional practical motivation for using $\mathrm{LG}_{0,\ell}$ modes in precision interferometers, including gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [119] [The beamformed trigger of RNO-G: its design and in-field performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24470)
*RNO-G Collaboration,:,S. Agarwal,J. A. Aguilar,N. Alden,S. Ali,P. Allison,M. Betts,D. Besson,A. Bishop,O. Botner,S. Bouma,S. Buitink,R. Camphyn,J. Chan,S. Chiche,B. A. Clark,K. Couberly,D. Dakroub,K. D. de Vries,C. Deaconu,P. Giri,C. Glaser,H. Gui,A. Hallgren,J. C. Hanson,S. Hassiki,K. Helbing,B. Hendricks,J. Henrichs,N. Heyer,C. Hornhuber,E. Huesca Santiago,K. Hughes,A. Jaitly,A. Karle,J. L. Kelley,C. Kopper,M. Korntheuer,M. Kowalski,I. Kravchenko,R. Krebs,M. Kugelmeier,D. Kullgren,R. Lahmann,C. -H. Liu,Y. Liu,M. J. Marsee,K. Mulrey,M. Muzio,A. Nelles,A. Novikov,A. Nozdrina,E. Oberla,N. Punsuebsay,M. Ravn,Z. Riesen,A. Rifaie,D. Ryckbosch,F. Schlüter,O. Scholten,P. Schriefer,D. Seckel,M. F. H. Seikh,Z. S. Selcuk,J. Stachurska,J. Stoffels,S. Toscano,D. Tosi,J. Tutt,N. van Eijndhoven,A. G. Vieregg,A. Vijai,H. Warnhofer,D. Washington,C. Welling,D. R. Williams,P. Windischhofer,S. Wissel,A. Zink*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了格陵兰无线电中微子观测站（RNO-G）新部署的相控阵（PA）触发系统，该系统基于延时求和波束形成和功率积分，显著提升了探测器对中微子诱导Askaryan信号的识别能力，并大幅增加了有效探测体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升RNO-G探测器对中微子诱导Askaryan信号的触发效率，抑制热噪声，增强低信噪比下的探测灵敏度。

Method: 设计并部署基于延时求和波束形成与功率积分的相控阵（PA）触发系统；通过实验室与实地测试脉冲及校准源表征性能；结合实测数据校准仿真模型，并用于计算有效体积。

Result: PA触发系统使触发所需信噪比平均降低25%；仿真与实测触发效率偏差在10%以内；有效体积在0.1 EeV以下提升超2倍，在100 EeV时至少提升1.3倍。

Conclusion: 新PA触发系统显著提升了RNO-G的探测性能与有效体积，验证了其在真实运行环境中的可靠性与仿真模型的准确性。

Abstract: The Radio Neutrino Observatory in Greenland (RNO-G) is a neutrino detector under construction at Summit Station, with 8 out of a planned 35 stations currently deployed. We have designed and deployed a new phased array (PA) trigger based on delay-and-sum beamforming and power integration. This trigger improves detector performance by suppressing thermal noise and better targeting neutrino-induced Askaryan signals. The new PA trigger has been deployed since the 2025 season. The trigger performance has been characterized using test pulses and calibration pulsers both in the lab and in-situ, and we find across these tests a 25% average reduction in the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) needed to trigger on signals. Simulations are shown to be representative of the detector, and simulated trigger efficiencies are within 10% of measured data. Following the in-situ trigger validation, we use data-driven trigger performance to inform simulations of the detector effective volumes. The PA trigger increases our effective volume significantly, by over a factor of 2 below 0.1 EeV and at least a factor of 1.3 at the highest energies of 100 EeV.

</details>


### [120] [Modelling Radio Frequency Interference on the Lunar Farside](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24527)
*Charlie K. Ashe,Ella J. Marshall,Evan F. Keane,Steve Prabu,Richard Lynch,David R. DeBoer*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了月球背面作为射电静默观测场所的可行性，指出尽管月球背面具有天然的射电宁静优势，但未来月球任务带来的有意和无意射频干扰（RFI）将显著增加，威胁其观测价值；通过建模分析，作者发现需至少30 dB的UEMR屏蔽才能保障类似LFT3望远镜的观测灵敏度，并强调应尽快开展相关科学测量并制定保护政策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着地面和轨道通信激增，射电天文观测面临日益严重的射频干扰（RFI）问题，月球背面因其天然屏蔽特性被视作理想观测场所；但新兴月球任务可能引入新的RFI，亟需评估其影响。

Method: 基于LFT3类仪器模型，结合规划中的月球任务目录及有意/无意射频发射模型，在0.1–2700 MHz频段内估算月球背面接收到的RFI强度与演化趋势。

Result: 有意RFI主要集中在2.4–2.6 GHz通信频段，而无意RFI广泛污染LFT3工作频带；为使最大干扰低于LFT3灵敏度，需至少30 dB的UEMR屏蔽；更灵敏望远镜需更高屏蔽要求；月球背面仍可用于LFT3类观测，但随月球卫星数量增长将日趋污染。

Conclusion: 月球背面射电静默窗口正快速收窄，必须加快相关科学测量，并在任务设计与政策层面加强月基射电科学环境的保护。

Abstract: Technological progress is a double-edged sword in the world of radio astronomy: improvements in antennas, receivers, and digital backends increase sensitivity, but rapid growth in terrestrial and orbital communications has caused a steep rise in radio-frequency interference (RFI). Modern datasets have become increasingly contaminated by RFI that is difficult to remove and can mimic or obscure astrophysical signals, complicating the search for objects such fast radio bursts (FRBs), pulsars, and technosignatures. The lunar farside has such been proposed as a site for radio-quiet observations, motivating missions such as the Lunar Farside Transients and Technology Telescope (LFT3) and others. However, planned lunar missions may introduce intended and unintended radio emissions into this protected environment. We use a LFT3-like instrument to model the evolution of the apparent lunar farside RFI environment over the coming years. Using a planned lunar mission catalogue and models for intended and unintended emissions, we estimate the received RFI over the 0.1-2700 MHz frequency range. We find that while intended emissions are concentrated near communications bands, particularly 2.4-2.6 GHz, unintended emissions contaminate broad regions of the LFT3 band. We further find that, under the assumptions of our model, uniform UEMR shielding of at least 30 dB is required to place the maximum interference below the sensitivity of a LFT3-like instrument; more sensitive telescopes would require higher UEMR shielding. We predict that the lunar farside will remain usable for LFT3-like observations, but will become increasingly contaminated as the lunar satellite population grows. This highlights the decreasing window of opportunity for measurements of the lunar farside's radio quiet environment and should inform future mission design and policy choices for the protection of Moon-based radio science.

</details>


### [121] [The Wide-field Spectroscopic Telescope (WST): design trade-offs for the low-resolution multi-object spectrograph instrument](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24546)
*Dimitri Buffat,Will Saunders,Kjetil Dohlen,Laurence Tresse,David Lee,Roland Bacon,Andrea Bianco,Laurane Fréour,Roelof de Jong,Etienne Burtin,Olaf Iwert,Virgile Meyer,Elizabeth George*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了宽视场光谱望远镜（WST）的低分辨率多目标光谱仪（MOS-LR）的设计与性能权衡，旨在实现前所未有的巡天效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对关键科学挑战，需建设具备大规模光谱巡天能力的12米级专用光谱设施，而现有设施巡天效率不足，亟需提升。

Method: 系统性地权衡光学与机械设计方案，综合考虑体积、质量约束，以及探测器和光栅等关键器件的可用性。

Result: 设计出覆盖3.1平方度视场、30,000目标复用能力、370–930 nm波段、分辨率为3000、光纤口径1角秒的MOS-LR仪器。

Conclusion: 该MOS-LR仪器将使WST巡天效率比当前设施提高一个数量级，但其设计、制造与维护面临极大挑战。

Abstract: The Wide-field Spectroscopic Telescope (WST) is a planned 12-meter-class dedicated spectroscopic facility designed to address key scientific challenges through large spectroscopic surveys. This paper presents design and performance trade-offs for the Low-Resolution Multi-Object Spectrograph (MOS-LR) instrument. With a multiplex of 30,000 covering a field of view of 3.1 square degrees, this instrument will provide unprecedented survey efficiency, an order of magnitude beyond those of current facilities. Covering the 370 to 930 nm range at a resolving power of 3,000 with a sky-projected fiber diameter of 1 arcsec, this instrument faces extreme challenges in design, manufacturing, and maintenance. We present a systematic approach to trading off optical and mechanical design options, taking into account constraints such as volume and mass, but also projected availability of detectors and gratings etc.

</details>


### [122] [Shack-Hartmann wavefront sensor slope measurement algorithm robust to scintillation and sensor saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24547)
*Timoth{é}e Vene,Laurent M Mugnier,Aur{é}lie Montmerle-Bonnefois,Jean-Marc Conan*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于联合最大似然估计的新型Shack-Hartmann波前传感器斜率测量算法，显著提升其在强闪烁和传感器饱和条件下的鲁棒性与可用光强范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量光学通信链路需在强大气扰动和低仰角条件下仍能有效使用自适应光学（AO）系统进行湍流补偿；而传统SHWFS算法在闪烁引起的强度波动下性能受限，尤其易受传感器饱和影响。

Method: 提出一种基于联合最大似然（joint maximum likelihood）的SHWFS斜率估计算法，兼顾光斑强度建模与斜率估计，增强对饱和和大动态范围强度变化的鲁棒性。

Result: 该算法对传感器饱和具有鲁棒性，可将可靠斜率测量的光强范围扩大两个数量级以上；通过提高分配给波前传感器的光强，可在饱和存在时仍显著增加有效测量次数。

Conclusion: 所提算法显著提升了SHWFS在强闪烁与低信噪比场景下的实用性，为高通量、低仰角光学通信中的自适应光学系统提供了更可靠、更宽动态范围的波前感知能力。

Abstract: High throughput optical communication links require the use of adaptive optics (AO) to compensate for atmospheric turbulence even in strong perturbation conditions and at low elevation. Considering the intensity fluctuations brought by scintillation, we present a new slope measurement algorithm for the Shack--Hartmann wavefront sensor (SHWFS), based on a joint maximum likelihood approach. We show that this estimator is robust to sensor saturation and widens by more than two orders of magnitude the intensity range exploitable for reliable slope measurements compared to classical algorithms. Consequently, by increasing the intensity assigned to the wavefront sensor, we also increase the number of valid measurements despite sensor saturation.

</details>


### [123] [Map Multi-Tool: A Map-Based Approach to Modeling Beam Systematics for Cosmic Microwave Background Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24605)
*Cesiley L. King,Alec Hryciuk,Jeff McMahon,Johanna M. Nagy,John E. Ruhl*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Map Multi-Tool（MMT）的新模拟框架，用于建模CMB仪器中与光束相关的系统误差，通过基于地图的方法高效评估其对宇宙学参数测量的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMB实验需精确模拟仪器系统误差以确保宇宙学参数测量的高保真度；量化这些效应有助于优化实验设计、设定性能指标并识别残余系统误差带来的偏差。

Method: 开发了基于地图的模拟框架MMT，将模拟天球信号与畸变的强度/偏振光束（含泄漏效应）做卷积，生成天空图和功率谱，作为宇宙学参数估计器的输入。

Result: 通过电读出串扰和探测器时间常数两类非理想光束实例验证了MMT能力：前者分析8种时分复用架构下的角功率谱泄漏差异；后者揭示时间常数不确定性对高l多极矩CMB谱及宇宙学参数的偏置影响。

Conclusion: MMT为CMB仪器关键设计与标定决策提供了有力工具，可有效支持系统误差的建模、评估与缓解。

Abstract: Cosmic microwave background (CMB) experiments use simulations of instrumental systematic effects to ensure high-fidelity measurements of cosmological parameters. Quantifying the expected magnitude of these effects enables experiments to improve designs, set performance requirements, and understand potential measurement biases from residual systematics. Here we present a new simulation framework, called Map Multi-Tool (MMT), which models beam-related systematics for CMB instruments using a map-based approach. The pipeline convolves simulated sky realizations with distorted intensity and polarization beams, including leakage effects, to produce sky maps and power spectra. These outputs can then be used as inputs for cosmological parameter estimators. this framework enables efficient evaluation of such systematic effects. We demonstrate the capabilities of MMT with examples of non-ideal beams induced by electrical readout crosstalk and detector time constant response. The electrical crosstalk example considers eight different schemes for a time-division multiplexed readout architecture, and shows how they lead to different levels of angular power spectrum leakage for row-switching and inductive crosstalk. The detector time constant example demonstrates how associated uncertainties can alter CMB spectra at high multipoles and bias cosmological parameters. These examples illustrate some of MMT's broad capabilities to inform critical design and calibration decisions to mitigate systematic effects in CMB instruments.

</details>


### [124] [Demonstrating the integration of a photonic lantern with an all-fiber-based nulling interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24737)
*Jordan Diaz,Rebecca Jensen-Clem,Philip M. Hinz,Daren Dillon,Pradip Gatkine,Aditya R. Sengupta,Dan Sirbu,Sarah Tedder,Kevin Bundy,Brian Vyhnalek,Steph Sallum,Matthew C. DeMartino,Stephen Eikenberry,Peter Delfyett,Rodrigo Amezcua-Correa*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种基于光子灯笼（PL）的全光纤焦平面消零干涉仪，用于高对比度成像太阳系尺度的系外行星和原行星，实验在1550 nm波长下使用商用组件完成，并验证了PL在光耦合及空间滤波方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高对比度成像技术在小角距下抑制恒星光能力不足，亟需新型仪器提升对太阳系尺度系外行星和原行星的探测能力。

Method: 构建并测试一种全光纤焦平面消零干涉仪，采用商用组件在1550 nm工作；对比光子灯笼耦合与激光直连两种输入方式的性能。

Result: 成功实现基于光子灯笼的光纤消零干涉仪，验证其有效耦合与空间滤波能力，并表明其适用于光子学科学仪器；提出该方案可拓展用于吸积原行星探测。

Conclusion: 光子灯笼为焦平面消零干涉提供了可行且有前景的光纤化路径，有望推动下一代高对比度成像仪器发展。

Abstract: High-contrast imaging of Solar System scale exoplanets and protoplanets demands advancements in instrumentation to access deeper starlight suppression at smaller angular separations than today's state-of-the-art. The multi-mode to single-mode conversion capabilities of photonic lanterns (PLs) provide new avenues to implement techniques such as nulling interferometry due to the inherent spatial filtering of single-mode waveguides. In this work, we present laboratory results on an all-fiber-based focal plane nulling interferometer using off-the-shelf components operating at 1550 nm. We demonstrate the implementation of a PL for coupling light into the instrument, and compare it to the case when laser light is directly fed into the interferometer. The integration of a PL with the interferometer evidences their potential for feeding photonic-based science instruments. Additionally, we discuss expanding the concept of the instrument for the detection of accreting protoplanets.

</details>

### [125] [First constraints on the nonperturbative gluon Collins-Soper kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24587)
*Artur Avkhadiev,Yang Fu,Phiala E. Shanahan,Michael L. Wagman,Yong Zhao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文首次在非微扰区域约束了胶子Collins-Soper核，利用格点QCD在接近物理π介子质量下进行计算，并结合大动量有效理论进行匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶子Collins-Soper核对理解横动量依赖分布的快度演化至关重要，但其非微扰行为此前缺乏直接约束。

Method: 采用格点QCD模拟（π介子质量≈172 MeV，格距a=0.15 fm），结合大动量有效理论（NNLL匹配）提取胶子Collins-Soper核。

Result: 首次获得q_T ∈ [300 MeV, 1.3 GeV]区间内非微扰胶子Collins-Soper核的定量约束。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来在QCD框架内系统确定胶子Collins-Soper核奠定基础，对胶子结构相关的实验具有重要启示。

Abstract: The gluon Collins-Soper kernel, which encodes the rapidity evolution of transverse-momentum-dependent gluon distributions, is constrained for the first time in the nonperturbative regime, for transverse momentum scales $q_{T} \in [ 300\text{ MeV}, 1.3\text{ GeV}]$. The constraints are determined in lattice QCD at a close-to-physical pion mass $M_π= 172(3)\text{ MeV}$, a single lattice spacing $a=0.15\text{ fm}$, and next-to-next-to-leading logarithmic matching in Large-Momentum Effective Theory. These results represent the first step toward a controlled determination of the gluon Collins-Soper kernel in QCD, with eventual phenomenological import and relevance to present and future experiments sensitive to the gluon structure of hadronic matter.

</details>


### [126] [Analytic decomposition of two-body electroweak processes with left-hand cuts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24648)
*André Baião-Raposo,Raúl A. Briceño,Nicolas Lang,Felipe G. Ortega-Gama,Bianca Pol*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有左手奇点（如单π交换）的电弱2+J→2强子跃迁振幅的壳上表示，将电弱流作为微扰处理，而强子相互作用则全阶求解；结果包含对数奇点、单粒子极点及三角奇点，并用纯强子振幅、交换流核、三角函数和一类短程跃迁函数表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为约束涉及核子或矢量介子（尤其重夸克区）系统的形状因子，需处理靠近阈值的单π交换奇点。

Method: 将电弱流作为外部场微扰处理（只保留领头阶），强子相互作用则全阶求解（含单粒子交换效应），并分离各类奇点贡献（对数、单粒子极点、三角奇点）。

Result: 得到壳上振幅表达式，由纯强子振幅、交换电流核、三角函数和实且光滑的短距跃迁函数组成；识别出对数奇点与单粒子极点等关键奇异性。

Conclusion: 该工作为处理重夸克系统中近阈值单π交换奇点提供了重要理论基础，是迈向核子或矢量介子系统形状因子严格约束的关键一步。

Abstract: We derive an on-shell representation for electroweak $2+J\to2$ transition amplitudes involving two hadrons in both the initial and final state for systems where there are left-hand singularities generated by light-particle exchange, e.g. one-pion exchange. The derivation treats the insertion of the electroweak current perturbatively, keeping only the leading-order contribution in the external field, while the hadronic interactions are treated to all orders, including one-particle exchange effects. We find that, in such processes, the amplitude can have logarithmic singularities, as well as one-particle pole singularities, due to these exchanges. We isolate those contributions, as well as previously identified triangle singularities. The result is expressed in terms of the purely hadronic amplitude, exchange-current kernels, triangle functions, and a class of short-distance transition functions that are real and smooth below unaccounted-for thresholds. While we consider only transitions with spinless particles, this work is an important step toward constraining form factors of systems involving nucleons or vector mesons in the heavy quark sector, where the one-pion-exchange singularity is quite close to threshold.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Effects of flavor-mixings on charged kaon and pion parton distribution functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23497)
*Fabio L. Braghin,Parada T. P. Hutauruk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在U(3) NJL模型中研究带电K介子和π介子的部分子分布函数（PDF），引入一种源于真空极化的新型味混合相互作用，并采用固有时间正规化方案，有效考虑夸克禁闭效应；计算了包含味混合相互作用下的能隙方程、介子质量及介子-夸克耦合常数，并得到介子价夸克PDF，与实验数据及JAM分析结果比较，发现味混合效应对PDF的影响依赖于夸克有效质量和其差异，并在特定x值处显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索带电介子（π和K）的价夸克部分子分布函数（PDF），并评估新型味混合相互作用对PDF的影响，该相互作用源自真空极化，不同于't Hooft瞬子诱导相互作用。

Method: 在U(3) Nambu-Jona-Lasinio (NJL) 模型框架下，引入新型味混合相互作用，采用固有时间正规化方案，求解gap方程，计算介子质量与介子-夸克耦合常数，并据此推导π和K介子的价夸克PDF。

Result: 发现味混合效应对PDF的影响强度正比于夸克有效质量及其差值；在x≈0.3和0.8处对π介子上夸克PDF影响显著，在x≈0.2和0.7处对K介子PDF影响显著；该效应在μ²=4和27 GeV²两个能标下强度相近，但整体改善了PDF与实验及JAM分析的一致性。

Conclusion: 新型味混合相互作用对介子PDF具有可观测影响，尤其在特定x区域，且固有时间正规化方案有助于更合理地描述夸克禁闭效应，提升理论预测与实验数据的符合度。

Abstract: We investigate the charged kaon and pion parton distribution functions (PDFs) in the U(3) Nambu--Jona-Lasinio (NJL) model, considering a novel flavor-mixing interaction arising from vacuum polarization that differs from the instanton-induced 't~Hooft interaction. In this work, the proper-time regularization scheme is employed, and, effectively, it might take quark confinement into account. The gap equations, the meson masses, and the meson--quark coupling constants are calculated in the presence of flavor mixing interactions. The valence-quark distributions of the charged kaon and pion are calculated and compared with existing experimental data and JAM analysis results at scales $μ^{2} =$ 4 and 27 GeV$^{2}$. The strength of mixing effects on the PDFs is found to be proportional to the quark effective masses and their differences. We further find that the dominant flavor mixing effects in the pion valence up-quark distribution at scales $μ^{2} =$ 27 and 4 GeV$^2$ are around $x \simeq 0.3$ and $x \simeq 0.8$, while for the kaon, the effects are shown at around $x \simeq 0.2$ and $x \simeq 0.7$. We also find similar strength of mixing effects on the PDFs at both scales $μ^{2} =4$ and 27 GeV$^{2}$; however, it certainly improves the pion and kaon PDFs.

</details>


### [128] [Electromagnetic form factors of singly charmed baryons $Σ_c$ and $Λ_c$ in a covariant quark-diquark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23943)
*Ye Cao,Cheng Chen,Dongyan Fu,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在协变夸克-双夸克模型框架下，系统研究了单粲重子Σc和Λc+的类空电磁形状因子，计算了其磁矩及电荷、磁矩半径，并揭示了粲夸克与轻双夸克对结果的影响机制；发现其电形状因子随动量转移衰减更慢，表明电荷分布更紧凑；磁结构上Σc由轻轴矢量双夸克主导，而Λc+则反常地由粲夸克主导；最后基于经验渐近关系预测了e+e−→ΣcΣ̄c总截面，可供BESIII、Belle II等实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解单粲重子（Σc和Λc+）的内部结构与动力学机制，特别是其电磁形状因子、磁矩及电荷/磁矩半径等可观测量，以深入认识重味强子的非微扰QCD性质。

Method: 采用协变夸克-双夸克模型，系统计算单粲重子Σc（++/+ /0）和Λc+的类空电磁形状因子，并结合经验渐近关系外推至类时区域以预测e+e−→ΣcΣ̄c反应的总截面。

Result: 1）Σc和Λc+的磁矩、电荷与磁矩半径被成功计算；2）电形状因子比质子衰减更慢，反映更紧凑的电荷分布（源于粲夸克局域化核心效应）；3）Σc磁结构由轻轴矢量双夸克主导，而Λc+磁结构反常地由粲夸克主导（因标量双夸克无贡献）；4）预测了e+e−→ΣcΣ̄c的总截面。

Conclusion: 轻双夸克的自旋构型（轴矢量vs标量）对单粲重子磁性质起决定性作用；粲夸克不仅提供质量尺度，还直接参与Λc+的磁矩生成；理论结果与格点QCD定性一致，且可被当前及未来实验检验。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of the spacelike electromagnetic form factors of the ground-state singly charmed baryons, $Σ_c$ ($Σ_c^{++},Σ_c^+,Σ_c^0$) and $Λ_c^+$, within a covariant quark-diquark model. Based on this framework, we obtain their magnetic moments as well as electric charge and magnetic moment radii. Such observables are important to understand the internal structure and the inner dynamics of these heavy baryon states. Our theoretical calculations are in qualitative agreement with available lattice QCD results of $Σ_c^{++}$ and $Σ_c^0$ baryons. We also discuss the mechanism behind the dependence of the numerical results on the charm quark and light diquark. A key finding is that the electric form factors of the singly charmed baryons fall off much more slowly with momentum transfer $Q^2$ than that of the proton, indicating a more compact electric charge distribution, which is attributed to the heavy charm quark acting as a localized core. More importantly, we observe a striking difference in the magnetic structure: while the magnetic form factors of $Σ_c$ are dominated by the light axial-vector diquark, those of $Λ_c^+$ are unexpectedly governed by the charm quark due to the vanishing contribution of the scalar diquark. This highlights the decisive role of the light-diquark spin configuration in determining the magnetic properties. Finally, using an empirical asymptotic relation connecting the spacelike and timelike regions, we predict the total cross section for $e^+e^-\toΣ_c\barΣ_c$. Our findings can be tested at existing facilities including the BESIII, Belle II and LHCb, as well as the proposed Super Tau-Charm Facility.

</details>


### [129] [Application of flavor moonshine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22744)
*Hirotaka Sugawara*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了先前关于月光理论在味物理中应用的工作，引入超对称性和模对称性，利用不同阶的双值模函数计算轻子、夸克及其超伴子的质量，并预测若干可被现有加速器（如KEKB和LHC）探测的超粒子质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展先前基于月光理论的味物理研究，纳入超对称性与模对称性以更系统地解释基本粒子质量谱，并实现可检验的预言。

Method: 采用K=1、2、3的双值模函数分别描述带电轻子、中微子与上夸克、下夸克；结合超对称框架，仅用每个代的一个输入质量参数推导全部费米子及对应超伴子质量。

Result: 给出超电子质量Me,s≈847 MeV、超奇异夸克质量≈5.56 GeV、超底夸克质量≈2193.4 GeV等具体数值预言，其中部分质量可在KEKB或LHC实验中检验。

Conclusion: 该模型通过模对称性与超对称性的结合，以极简输入实现对标准模型粒子及超伴子质量的统一描述，并提供若干可实验验证的关键预言。

Abstract: In our previous paper [1], we presented the application of Moonshine to the flavor physics based on the work of Cohn and Deutsch [2] and Bagger and West. Conway and S. Norton [3] and T.Eguchi, H.Ooguri and Y.Tachikawa[4]. We present here the extension of our previous paper [1] by including the supersymmetry and the modular symmetry. We calculate all the lepton masses and the quark masses and their super partner masses by using the one input particle mass for each family. The two valued modular function is defined in equation(7). We define the two valued modular function of degree K in equation (7). We use K = 1 two valued modular function for the charged leptons and k = 2 two valued modular function for the neutrino and for the up quarks and k = 3 two valued modular function for the down quarks. We emphasize that we have three particle masses which can be measured by the existing accelerators such as KEK B factory or CERN LHC. One is super electron mass "Me,s =847 MeV" which is slightly lower than the proton mass. The Super d, s and b quark masses are 0 GeV, 5.56 GeV, and 2193.4 GeV. Super down quark mass is 0 and super strange quark mass can be measured both in KEKB and in CERN LHC. The super bottom quark mass can be measured only in LHC.

</details>


### [130] [Axion isocurvature and the model-building problem of low-scale inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22809)
*Sarunas Verner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了轻轴子在暴胀时期的各向异性（isocurvature）限制，独立处理暴胀期归一化衰变常数 f_I 和晚期衰变常数 f_a，并考虑了任意暗物质占比、扰动传递及非谐波丰度响应；结果对暴胀哈勃参数 H_I 给出严格上限，并与张量谱联合给出轴子-张量及轴子条件Lyth界限，进而约束暴胀场位移与势函数导数，支持具有独立调控能力的暴胀模型（如混合型、跑质量型、拐点型），并指出再加热与PQ对称性不恢复会进一步收紧约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上认为QCD轴子需低能标暴胀，但该文旨在建立更普适、更严格的各向异性约束框架，涵盖轴子归一化、丰度生成机制与扰动演化的自由度，以更准确评估轴子作为暗物质候选者的可行性及其对暴胀模型的限制。

Method: 基于尺度不变谱的轻轴子模型，独立设定暴胀期衰变常数 f_I 与晚期 f_a，引入任意初始倾角 θ_i、非谐波修正、非100%暗物质占比以及一般扰动转移函数；结合CMB观测（尤其是各向异性功率谱）与张量谱联合分析，推导 H_I 上限，并进一步导出对暴胀势 V(φ) 及其一、二阶导数的约束，提出轴子-张量和轴子条件Lyth界限。

Result: 在标准基准下（f_I=f_a≤M_Pl，轴子占全部DM，角扰动守恒），CMB各向异性给出95%置信度上限 H_I < 1.25×10¹⁰ GeV；当 θ_i=1 时收紧至 H_I < 2.2×10⁷ GeV；联合张量谱后，得到 Δφ_CMB/M_Pl ≲ 10⁻⁷–10⁻⁴ 和 |M_Pl V'/V| ∼ 10⁻⁸–10⁻⁵、M_Pl² V''/V ∼ −10⁻²；再加热与PQ非恢复进一步强化约束，而非最小模型可缓解之。

Conclusion: 轴子各向异性约束远比以往认知更严格且模型依赖性强；它强烈偏好能独立调控暴胀能标、势函数导数、退出机制与再加热的暴胀模型；同时凸显了早期宇宙物理（如PQ对称性破缺历史）与晚期宇宙学观测（CMB、引力波）之间的深层耦合。

Abstract: The pre-inflationary QCD axion is often said to require low-scale inflation. We derive a general isocurvature bound for a light axion with a scale-invariant spectrum, treating its inflationary normalization $f_I$ independently of the late-time decay constant $f_a$ and allowing for an arbitrary axion dark matter fraction, nontrivial perturbation transfer, and the full anharmonic abundance response. In the minimal benchmark, where axions constitute all of the dark matter, $f_I=f_a\leq M_{\rm Pl}$, and the angular perturbation is conserved, the least restrictive CMB limit considered gives the 95% C.L. upper bound $H_I<1.25\times10^{10}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ on the inflationary Hubble scale. For an initial misalignment angle $θ_i=1$, the bound strengthens to $H_I<2.2\times10^7\,\mathrm{GeV}$, with still stronger constraints near the hilltop. Combining the isocurvature and tensor spectra yields axion-tensor and axion-conditioned Lyth bounds. If the observed curvature perturbation is generated by a canonical cold single-field slow-roll inflaton and the tensor amplitude varies slowly across the observable CMB window, these relations limit the inflaton excursion to $Δφ_{\rm CMB}/M_{\rm Pl}\lesssim10^{-7}\text{-}10^{-4}$ and require $|M_{\rm Pl}V'/V|\sim10^{-8}\text{-}10^{-5}$ while $M_{\rm Pl}^2V''/V\sim-10^{-2}$. This hierarchy favors models with independent control of the inflationary scale, potential derivatives, exit, and reheating, including hybrid, running-mass, and inflection-point models. Reheating and the requirement of Peccei-Quinn non-restoration sharpen these conditions, while nonminimal scenarios can relax them by modifying the primordial fluctuation, its transfer, or the relic abundance.

</details>


### [131] [Agentic Re-Casting using Agentic Re-Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22813)
*Sascha Diefenbacher,Tilman Plehn,Daniel Schiller,Nikita Schmal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MadAgents.v3的智能代理系统，辅助物理学家在环形对撞机（LHC）上高效更新SFitter全局分析，使先进方法更易普及，并具备向其他工具推广的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHC上的分析重铸高度标准化但耗时耗力，需大量资源与物理专业知识；本文旨在降低SFitter等先进分析工具的使用门槛。

Method: 基于MadAgents.v3构建带物理学家参与的智能代理系统，实现SFitter全局分析的自动化更新与交互式重铸。

Result: 成功实现了SFitter分析的智能化、交互式更新，验证了该代理框架的可行性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该代理系统显著提升了LHC数据分析效率与可及性，其架构可轻松泛化至其他高能物理分析框架。

Abstract: Analysis re-casting at the LHC is highly standardized and nevertheless requires resources, time, and physics input. Building on the new MadAgents.v3, we show how a global SFitter analysis can be updated by an agentic system with a physicist in the loop. The agentic interface allows us to make the advanced SFitter methodology available to a wider audience. All physical and technical aspects of this agentic re-casting study can be trivially generalized beyond SFitter.

</details>


### [132] [$\texttt{SNuDD}$: Solar Neutrinos for Direct Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22817)
*Dorian W. P. Amaral,David Cerdeño,Andrew Cheek,Valeria Costa,Patrick Foldenauer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SNuDD——一个开源Python软件包，用于计算直接探测实验中的太阳中微子率谱，并利用该工具对中性流非标准相互作用（NSI）耦合给出新的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前直接探测实验正逐渐具备与专用中微子实验相媲美的灵敏度，亟需统一处理太阳中微子信号的工具，以将直接探测纳入中微子物理全局拟合框架。

Method: 开发了开源Python软件包SNuDD，支持标准模型及超出标准模型（如中性流NSI）情形下的核/电子反冲微分事例率计算；考虑了太阳与地球中传播的物质效应，以及散射截面在探测点的修正；结合LZ、XENONnT、PandaX-4T实验数据及XLZD/PandaX-xT未来规划开展分析。

Result: 利用现有氙基实验给出了NSI有效耦合的新限制；预测未来氙探测器可提供领先约束；指出当前直接探测实验灵敏度已快速逼近专用中微子实验。

Conclusion: SNuDD为整合直接探测与中微子实验数据提供了关键工具，建议在未来的中微子物理全局拟合中广泛采用。

Abstract: We introduce Solar Neutrinos for Direct Detection ($\texttt{SNuDD}$): an open-source Python package that enables the computation of the solar neutrino rate spectrum at direct detection experiments. $\texttt{SNuDD}$ can be used to determine the differential rate for both nuclear and electron recoils within the Standard Model and in the presence of beyond Standard Model physics effects, such as those arising from neutral-current non-standard interactions (NSI). The package accounts for matter effects during neutrino propagation through both the Sun and the Earth and for modifications to the scattering cross sections at the interaction site. We employ $\texttt{SNuDD}$ to place new limits on the effective NSI couplings using results from the xenon-based direct detection experiments LZ, XENONnT, and PandaX-4T, and we project the sensitivity of a future xenon detector based on the planned XLZD and PandaX-xT observatories. We find that current direct detection experiments are rapidly approaching sensitivities comparable to those of dedicated neutrino experiments and that future xenon detectors can provide leading constraints. We recommend that $\texttt{SNuDD}$ be used to combine incoming direct detection data with those from neutrino experiments in future global fits, placing direct detection within the broader landscape of neutrino physics.

</details>


### [133] [Properties of $Ω_{ccb}$ and $Ω_{bbc}$ baryons in a quark-diquark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23072)
*Chaitanya Anil Bokade,Bhaghyesh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用相对论屏蔽势模型和夸克-双夸克近似，研究了Ω_ccb和Ω_bbc重子的质量谱和辐射跃迁，发现双夸克激发能量低于夸克与双夸克相对运动激发，且自旋翻转跃迁被压制，轨道或径向跃迁主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Ω_ccb和Ω_bbc三重重夸克重子的质量谱与辐射跃迁特性，检验重双夸克系统刚性假设，并为未来实验提供理论参考。

Method: 采用相对论屏蔽势模型结合夸克-双夸克近似，计算S、P、D波激发态质量及辐射跃迁宽度。

Result: 发现双夸克内部激发能量低于夸克-双夸克相对运动激发；自旋翻转跃迁被压制，而轨道/径向跃迁主导且衰变宽度更大。

Conclusion: 该模型揭示了三重重夸克重子中双夸克动力学的新特征，挑战了传统刚性双夸克图像，对理解其内部结构及指导实验探测具有重要价值。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the mass spectra and radiative transitions of $Ω_{ccb}$ and $Ω_{bbc}$ baryons within a relativistic screened potential model using the quark-diquark approximation to capture key aspects of their internal structure. The model describes the $S$-, $P$-, and $D$-wave excitations, provides corresponding mass predictions, and offers a comparison with other theoretical approaches. Our findings suggest that excitations involving the diquark occur at lower energies than those requiring relative motion between the quark and diquark, challenging conventional assumptions about the rigidity of heavy diquark systems. Analysis of radiative decays of these states, shows that spin-flip transitions within the same orbital multiplet are suppressed, while transitions involving orbital or radial changes dominate with significantly larger widths. The model offers subtle distinctions in excitation dynamics and highlights its value for future studies and for interpreting potentially upcoming experimental results on triply heavy baryons.

</details>


### [134] [Exact Phenomenology of the Neutrino Cuboid: Normal Mass Ordering, the Tribimaximal Limit, and Cosmological Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23100)
*Jianlong Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于几何结构的中微子质量模型——'中微子立方体'，通过参数ξ和ζ将三个中微子质量与混合角θ₁₂、θ₂₃对齐，并导出不依赖于立方点展开的精确一致性关系；结果表明当前实验数据偏好正常质量序和θ₂₃处于低八分象限，但标准宇宙学模型下的中微子质量上限对该模型构成强约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个具有清晰几何意义且能自然容纳当前中微子振荡数据（如近似三最大混合）的质量模型，并检验其与绝对质量测量及宇宙学约束的兼容性。

Method: 定义由角度参数ξ、ζ刻画的中微子质量立方体结构，设定质量-混合角对齐条件（ξ=θ₁₂, ζ=θ₂₃），严格推导闭合形式的一致性关系；分析数值稳定性、质量序选择、θ₂₃取值范围，并结合JUNO模拟数据与ΛCDM宇宙学限制进行检验。

Result: 得出sin²θ₁₂ < 1/3 蕴含正常质量序；在该序下，Δm²₂₁/Δm²₃₁ ∈ (0,1) 要求θ₂₃位于低八分象限（sin²θ₂₃ ≈ 0.440）；JUNO基准输入给出各质量约为(0.0938, 0.0942, 0.1063) eV，总和约0.294 eV；一阶展开在质量劈裂比上数值不稳定；当前大气角数据仅轻微偏离精确对齐，但ΛCDM宇宙学质量上限强烈排斥该模型。

Conclusion: 中微子立方体模型在振荡现象学上具吸引力且可检验，但需扩展宇宙学模型才可能缓解其与绝对质量限制的张力；提出的几何对齐残差可作为未来中微子实验的通用零检验观测量。

Abstract: We investigate the exact phenomenology of a geometric neutrino cuboid defined by $m_1=m_0\sinξ$, $m_2=m_0\cosξ\sinζ$, and $m_3=m_0\cosξ\cosζ$, whose cubic point corresponds to mass degeneracy and the tribimaximal values of the solar and atmospheric mixing angles. Imposing the mass-mixing alignment $ξ=θ_{12}$ and $ζ=θ_{23}$, we derive closed-form consistency relations without relying on an expansion about the cubic limit. These relations show that the observed condition $\sin^2θ_{12}<1/3$ selects normal mass ordering and, in the resulting normal-ordering regime, the physical condition $0<Δm_{21}^2/Δm_{31}^2<1$ requires $θ_{23}$ to lie in the lower octant. Representative JUNO-based inputs give $\sin^2θ_{23}\simeq0.440$, $(m_1,m_2,m_3)\simeq(0.0938,0.0942,0.1063)\mathrm{eV}$, and $\sum_i m_i\simeq0.294\mathrm{eV}$. We demonstrate that the first-order expansion around the tribimaximal point is numerically unstable for the solar-to-atmospheric mass-splitting ratio because of a leading-order cancellation. Present atmospheric-angle data yield only mild tension with exact alignment, whereas stringent neutrino-mass bounds in baseline $Λ$CDM cosmology strongly disfavor it; its viability under extended cosmological models remains model dependent. We finally formulate geometric alignment residuals as general null-test observables, providing a systematic framework for testing the neutrino cuboid with future oscillation and absolute-mass measurements.

</details>


### [135] [Testing selectively enhanced QCD axions couplings as an explanation of the RX J1856.5-3754 hard X-ray excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23140)
*Charul Rathod,Madhukar Mishra,P. K Das*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用QCD轴子转换光子解释Magnificent Seven中子星硬X射线观测数据的可能性，发现微电子伏特尺度下选择性增强耦合的QCD轴子模型比标准KSVZ模型更符合2–8 keV硬X射线超量数据，而毫电子伏特质量轴子在此框架下不可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Magnificent Seven中子星观测到的2–8 keV硬X射线超量现象，探索QCD轴子作为可能物理来源的可行性。

Method: 采用核子-核子轫致辐射作为基准轴子产生机制，并加入Cooper对破缺与形成（PBF）过程；比较标准KSVZ轴子模型与选择性增强耦合的QCD轴子模型对硬X射线数据的拟合能力。

Result: meV质量QCD轴子无法解释观测数据；微eV尺度的选择性增强耦合模型与硬X射线数据吻合更好。

Conclusion: 在增强耦合场景下，QCD轴子可合理解释M7孤立中子星在2–8 keV能段的硬X射线辐射。

Abstract: We explore the possibility of explaining hard X-ray data obtained from one of the Magnificent Seven (M7) neutron-stars (NSs) employing QCD axion-converted photons. The emission of thermal axions along with neutrinos from the core has been considered. We adopt the nucleon-nucleon bremsstrahlung process as the baseline axion production mechanism and Cooper-pair breaking and formation (PBF) as an additional process. We investigate here whether the selectively enhanced QCD axion coupling model can better explain the hard X-ray excess than the commonly used KSVZ axion model. Our results suggest that meV-mass QCD axions cannot explain the hard X-ray observation within the adopted framework. The enhanced selective model in the micro-eV scale provides closer agreement with the hard X-ray data. We thus conclude that the emission of hard X-rays in the $2-8$ keV range from isolated M7 stars could be explained by QCD axions under an enhanced coupling scenario.

</details>


### [136] [High-frequency gravitational waves from axion inflation in the weak-backreaction regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23152)
*Xun-Jie Xu,Junyu Zhu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轴子暴胀模型中弱反作用（WB）区域内的规范场放大和原初引力波（GW）产生，特别关注非瞬时再加热阶段，发现该机制可在高频段产生目前已知最强的原初GW信号之一，对$N_{\rm eff}$测量及高频GW探测器发展具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于强反作用（SB）区域以增强GW信号，但存在计算复杂性和标量扰动过量风险；本文旨在探索更理论安全的弱反作用（WB）区域，并填补非瞬时再加热阶段GW演化的研究空白。

Method: 通过连续追踪暴胀慢滚、再加热至辐射主导全过程，数值求解含Chern-Simons耦合的轴子-规范场系统动力学，重点分析再加热期 inflaton 振荡导致的不稳定性参数 $\xi$ 符号翻转及其对左右手螺旋规范场模的激发效应。

Result: 发现在WB区域内，再加热阶段因 $\xi$ 频繁变号可同时激发双螺旋规范场模，显著提升高频原初GW产额，其强度可达当前理论框架下最高水平之一，且信号集中在高频频段（可能对应 $N_{\rm eff}$ 精密测量与新型高频探测器敏感频带）。

Conclusion: 轴子暴胀在弱反作用区域结合非瞬时再加热可自然产生极强的高频原初引力波信号，既规避了强反作用区域的理论风险，又为未来宇宙学观测和探测器设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Axion inflation, characterized by a Chern-Simons interaction between the inflaton and a gauge field, provides a powerful mechanism for generating primordial gravitational waves (GWs) through tachyonic enhancement of the gauge field. While recent literature has predominantly focused on the Strong Backreaction (SB) regime to maximize GW signals for future interferometers, this regime suffers from computational complexities as well as the risk of overproducing scalar perturbations. In this work, we investigate gauge field amplification and GW production strictly within the theoretically safer Weak Backreaction (WB) regime, with a particular focus on the largely unexplored non-instantaneous reheating phase. Because the tachyonic enhancement during slow-roll typically increases as inflation approaches its end, it is crucial to investigate how the production of GWs behaves at the very end of inflation and thereafter. By continuously tracking the evolution from slow-roll through reheating to radiation domination, we present a complete picture of inflationary and post-inflationary GW production in this framework. A particularly interesting feature of the post-inflationary phase is that the oscillatory behavior of the inflaton during reheating leads to frequent sign-flips of the instability parameter $ξ$, exciting both helical modes of the gauge field. Our analysis reveals that axion inflation can naturally generate one of the strongest known primordial GW signals at high-frequency bands. The yield is relevant for future precision measurements of the effective number of neutrino species, $N_{\rm eff}$, and also strongly motivates the development of novel high-frequency GW detectors.

</details>


### [137] [Quasi-two-body decays $B_c^+ \to χ_{c0,c1} [ρ(K^*) \to] ππ(Kπ)$ in the PQCD approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23163)
*Jun Deng,Xian-Qiao Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在微扰QCD框架下研究了B_c^+介子到χ_{c0,c1}与ρ/K^*共振态（随后衰变到ππ/Kπ）的准双体衰变过程，计算了分支比、极化参数及不同通道间的比率，并指出ρ共振态干涉和共振质量对结果有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解B_c^+介子衰变中P波粲偶素（χ_{c0}, χ_{c1}）与轻强子共振态（如ρ, K^*）联合产生的机制，特别是共振干涉、极化分布及夸克味因子（如Cabibbo抑制）的影响，以检验QCD因子化方法并解释实验观测中的相对产额异常。

Method: 采用微扰QCD（pQCD）因子化方法，在领阶（leading order）下计算B_c^+ → χ_{c0,c1} [ρ(K^*) → ππ(Kπ)] 的准双体衰变振幅；ρ和K^*共振态分别用Gounaris-Sakurai和相对论Breit-Wigner模型参数化；考虑多个ρ激发态（ρ(770), ρ(1450), ρ(1700)）及其干涉效应；提取分支比、纵向极化分数f_L及通道比率R。

Result: 主导的ρ(770)通道分支比为：B(B_c^+→χ_{c0}ππ)=3.24×10^{-3}，B(B_c^+→χ_{c1}ππ)=4.19×10^{-3}；ρ干涉贡献约25%；Kπ通道受Cabibbo抑制至~10^{-6}；χ_{c1}衰变中纵向极化f_L≈94%，随共振质量升高而下降；R_{χ_{c1}/χ_{c0}}^{ππ}≈1.30，远低于纯两体衰变预期的~4.7；R_{K/π}≈2×10^{-3}在χ_{c0}与χ_{c1}间一致。

Conclusion: ρ共振态的产生及干涉显著压低χ_{c1}/χ_{c0}产额比，表明准双体衰变动力学与简单两体图像迥异；pQCD能合理描述此类含共振的非轻强子衰变；结果可为LHCb等实验检验提供理论基准。

Abstract: We study the quasi-two-body decays $B_c^+ \to χ_{c0,c1} [ρ(K^*) \to] π^+π^0(K^0π^+)$ in the perturbative QCD framework at leading order. The $ππ$ and $Kπ$ pairs are produced via the P-wave resonances $ρ(770)$, $ρ(1450)$, $ρ(1700)$, and $K^*(892)$, parametrized by the Gounaris-Sakurai and relativistic Breit-Wigner models.
  The dominant $ρ(770)$ channels yield $\mathcal{B}(B_c^+ \to χ_{c0} π^+π^0) = 3.24 \times 10^{-3}$ and $\mathcal{B}(B_c^+ \to χ_{c1} π^+π^0) = 4.19 \times 10^{-3}$, with constructive $ρ$ interference contributing $\sim 25\%$ of the total. The $Kπ$ channels are Cabibbo-suppressed to $10^{-6}$. For $χ_{c1}$ modes, $f_L \approx 94\%$ dominates and decreases with increasing resonance mass. The ratio $R_{χ_{c1}/χ_{c0}}^{ππ} \approx 1.30$ contrasts with the two-body result $\sim 4.7$, showing that resonant $ρ$ production dramatically reshapes the relative yields of P-wave charmonia. The ratio $R_{K/π} \approx 2\times10^{-3}$ is consistent across $χ_{c0}$ and $χ_{c1}$.

</details>


### [138] [Novel Multilepton Signatures from the Fermionic Portal to Vector Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23166)
*Alexander Belyaev,Manimala Chakraborti,Claire Shepherd-Themistocleous,Chang-Yuan Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于六缪子末态的新探测策略，用于寻找通过暗区级联衰变产生的矢量类轻子，该信号具有重复的双缪子、三缪子和五缪子共振结构，现有LHC多轻子搜索未利用该特征，新分析可在HL-LHC上将矢量类缪子质量探测极限提升至约1.9 TeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LHC多轻子搜索未充分利用六缪子末态中特有的重复共振结构（如双缪子、三缪子、五缪子），而该结构在矢量类轻子对产生及暗区级联衰变中显著，有望大幅提升探测灵敏度。

Method: 提出基于拓扑结构的重建方法，利用信号中重复出现的dimuon、trimuon和five-muon共振特征；在探测器水平模拟信号与标准模型背景（特别处理重味缪子）；设计六缪子选择与拓扑重建流程。

Result: 六缪子末态背景极低；Run-2多轻子搜索仅部分约束低质量参数空间；专用六缪子搜索可显著扩展参数空间覆盖，在HL-LHC下可达矢量类缪子质量约1.9 TeV。

Conclusion: 六缪子末态是探测矢量类轻子及暗区级联衰变的理想通道，所提出的拓扑重建方法能有效压低背景并提升灵敏度，为未来HL-LHC实验提供新搜索方向。

Abstract: We perform a collider study of a novel multilepton signature arising from pair production of heavy vector-like leptons followed by cascade decays through a dark sector. In the muonic realisation of the Fermionic Portal to Vector Dark Matter, this process can lead to final states with four, six, eight, or ten visible muons, depending on the dark-sector spectrum and branching pattern. We identify the six-muon channel as the most powerful target: it remains sizeable over a broad region of parameter space, while being less rate-suppressed than the higher-multiplicity channels and much cleaner and more reconstructable than the four-muon final state. The signal arises from Drell--Yan pair production of vector-like muons, $pp\toμ'^{+}μ'^{-}$, followed by decays through the dark vector $V'$ and the dark scalar $H_D$. The six-muon final state receives contributions from symmetric decay topologies in which each $μ'$ yields three visible muons, and from the asymmetric topology in which one chain yields one muon and the other yields five. The six-lepton signature from vector-like-lepton pair production has not previously been explored at the LHC. We therefore develop a topology-based reconstruction which exploits the repeated dimuon, trimuon, and five-muon resonance structure of the signal. We simulate signal and Standard Model backgrounds at detector level, including a dedicated treatment of rare heavy-flavour muons. The resulting background after the six-muon selection and topology reconstruction is negligible. Existing Run-2 multilepton searches already constrain part of the low-mass parameter space, but they do not exploit the repeated resonance structure of the signal. A dedicated six-muon search can substantially extend the reach. At the HL-LHC, the proposed analysis can probe vector-like muon masses up to about $1.9$ TeV for favourable spectra.

</details>


### [139] [A Chromomagnetic Mechanism for the Rotational Phase Transition of Gluonic Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23199)
*Zhibin Li,Yidian Chen,Mei Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过全息框架下的旋转-磁场对应关系，研究纯胶子物质在旋转下的响应，发现真实旋转增强色磁弦张力并提高退禁闭温度；低温下弱弦张力导致负Barnett效应，略高于相变温度时自旋主导产生异常负转动惯量，该异常仅存在于弱实旋转下，高温下恢复常规Barnett行为；其微观机制是色磁诱导的惯性反转（CII）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转作为QCD物质的关键调控参数，但有效模型与格点QCD对其对退禁闭相变影响的预测存在冲突，亟需厘清旋转对纯胶子物质的微观影响机制。

Method: 基于全息框架中的旋转-磁场对应，结合格点QCD在虚角速度下的数据校准，研究实旋转下色磁弦张力、退禁闭温度及Barnett响应等物理量的变化规律。

Result: 实旋转增强色磁弦张力、提高退禁闭温度；低温出现负Barnett效应，略高于相变温度出现异常负转动惯量（源于自旋主导），该异常仅存于弱旋转区域；高温下恢复常规Barnett行为；提出色磁诱导惯性反转（CII）作为微观机制。

Conclusion: 色磁通管的熔解与热恢复主导旋转响应，CII机制揭示了QCD物质中由非微扰色磁结构引发的异常转动惯量反转现象，统一解释了格点QCD的解析延拓预测与实验可观测量之间的联系。

Abstract: Rotation serves as a pivotal control parameter for QCD matter, yet effective models and lattice QCD yield conflicting predictions regarding its effect on the deconfinement transition. Using a rotation-magnetic correspondence within a holographic framework, we investigate the rotational response of pure gluonic matter. Calibrated against lattice QCD data at imaginary angular velocity, we find real rotation enhances chromomagnetic string tension and raises deconfinement temperature, consistent with lattice QCD analytic-continuation predictions. The temperature dependence of chromomagnetic string tension dominates the system's Barnett response: weak low-temperature tension induces the negative Barnett effect, and slightly above the transition, spin contributions prevail to generate an anomalous negative total moment of inertia. Since growing angular velocity further strengthens chromomagnetic string tension and suppresses spin-dominated inversion, this anomalous regime only survives at weak real rotation and vanishes at large angular velocity. At high temperature, fully restored strong string tension stabilizes conventional Barnett behavior. Stemming from the melting and thermal restoration of nonperturbative chromomagnetic flux tubes, our results establish the chromomagnetic-induced inertia inversion (CII) mechanism as the microscopic origin of this anomalous rotational response.

</details>


### [140] [Status of light inflaton: from inflation to laboratory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23200)
*Niloy Mondal,Shashwat Sharma,Mathew Thomas Arun,Basabendu Barman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了轻型暴胀子模型在最新阿塔卡马宇宙学望远镜（ACT）观测数据及对亚GeV质量弱耦合标量粒子的对撞机与强度前沿实验限制下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验轻型暴胀子模型在当前最严格宇宙学和粒子物理实验约束下的可行性，特别是结合ACT最新数据与多个实验对弱耦合轻标量的限制。

Method: 基于四次方暴胀子势，结合ACT的暴胀观测量约束，并综合NA62、KOTO、BaBar、Belle、LHCb、MATHUSLA、FASER2、SHiP及中性介子振荡等实验结果，推导暴胀子质量与暴胀子-希格斯混合角的联合约束；同时探讨该框架下暴胀后加热阶段产生暗物质的可能性。

Result: 确定了与ACT观测一致的参数空间区域，并给出了暴胀子质量和混合角的严格限制；发现该框架下可在满足暴胀观测量前提下实现可行的暗物质产生机制。

Conclusion: 轻型暴胀子模型在当前多方面实验约束下仍具可行性，但参数空间显著受限；其与暗物质产生的联系为后续实验探测提供了新方向。

Abstract: We investigate the viability of the light inflaton scenario in light of the latest inflationary constraints from the Atacama Cosmology Telescope (ACT), together with bounds from collider and intensity-frontier experiments searching for a feebly coupled light scalar with a sub-GeV mass. Assuming a quartic inflaton potential, we identify the region of parameter space consistent with the ACT observations and derive constraints on the inflaton mass and inflaton-Higgs mixing using results from NA62, KOTO, BaBar, Belle, LHCb, MATHUSLA, FASER2, SHiP, and neutral meson oscillations. We also explore the prospects for dark matter production during reheating within this framework, while remaining consistent with the inflationary observables.

</details>


### [141] [Faddeev description of baryons in two-dimensional QCD with $N_c=3$. I. Chiral spectrum, isospin, and strangeness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23435)
*Ismail Zahed,Haocheng Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在二维QCD中发展了基于光前Faddeev方法的重子描述，精确求解耦合通道，并验证了手征极限下存在无质量轻重子；结果与DLCQ、玻色化等方法高度一致，并首次在价夸克层次实现了QCD₂中超子谱学的完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维QCD中重子结构及其谱学性质，特别是手征对称性实现、多通道效应及与高维QCD现象（如超子质量顺序、Gell-Mann–Okubo和Coleman–Glashow关系）的对应关系。

Method: 采用光前Faddeev方程构建重子波函数，在N_c=3下进行精确耦合通道分析，并用变分法结合精确矩阵元求解；扩展至两味及三味（含奇异夸克）情形，系统研究质量谱与通道组成。

Result: 手征极限下存在严格无质量重子；激发态谱与Webber结果逐态吻合；多通道特性明确区分基态与激发态；首次在价夸克层次再现QCD₂中超子谱：Σ类低于Λ类、手征阈值含两个kaon单位、Gell-Mann–Okubo关系在质量平方上严格成立、Coleman–Glashow关系精度达1%。

Conclusion: 光前Faddeev方法能统一描述二维QCD中从手征对称性破缺到重子谱精细结构的全貌，揭示了重子多通道本质，并为理解真实QCD中的重子物理提供了可控理论参照。

Abstract: We develop a light-front Faddeev description of baryons in two-dimensional QCD at $N_c=3$, in which an exact coupled-channel representation identifies the quark--diquark picture as a single-channel truncation, and solve it variationally with exact matrix elements. In the chiral limit we find an exactly massless lightest baryon, in agreement with the classic valence solutions, DLCQ, and bosonization; the excitation tower reproduces Webber's spectrum state by state and sets the valence gap $1.371\,M^{\ast}_{\rm mes}$. The channel content distinguishes these states: the massless baryon is single-channel, the gapped states are genuinely multi-channel, and all baryon Regge trajectories are of the meson class. The calculation is anchored at both ends of the coupling range: the meson-to-baryon mass ratio agrees with strong-coupling bosonization to $0.3$--$4.3\%$, while the valence masses reproduce the full discretized light cone quantization (DLCQ) masses to $0.04\%$ (meson) and $0.15\%$ (baryon) at weak coupling. Extending to two degenerate flavors, we find the massless baryon to be the $Δ$-like quartet, while the $N$-like doublet at $0.977\,M^{\ast}_{\rm mes}$ is an exact $50\!:\!50$ superposition of good- and bad-diquark channels. Adding a massive strange quark, we obtain, to our knowledge for the first time at the valence three-quark level, the hyperon spectroscopy of QCD$_2$: $Σ$-like states fall below $Λ$-like ones, inverting the four-dimensional ordering, the chiral thresholds count two kaon units per strange quark, and the Gell-Mann--Okubo relation is protected in mass squared. With all three quark masses non-degenerate, the proton-like state falls below the neutron-like one, $Σ^0$--$Λ$ mixing appears, and the Coleman--Glashow relation holds at the percent level.

</details>


### [142] [Simultaneous Color Glass Condensate fit to deep inelastic scattering and forward hadron production at HERA, RHIC, and the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23485)
*Piotr Korcyl,Truong My Hau Le,Farid Salazar,Tomasz Stebel*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次同时拟合了来自HERA的深度非弹性散射（DIS）和来自RHIC及LHC的前向单包含强子产生（SIHP）数据，采用带跑动耦合的领头阶Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）演化方程描述偶极振幅，并引入常数K因子修正高阶效应，获得接近1的全局χ²/d.o.f.；SIHP数据有助于约束偶极振幅演化速度且与DIS结果无矛盾，K因子在RHIC约为LHC的两倍，与阈值重求和计算一致；碎片函数和因子化尺度是K因子的主要系统不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一描述DIS与前向强子产生以检验Color Glass Condensate理论框架下BK演化对不同能区、不同过程的普适性，并厘清高阶修正与系统误差的影响。

Method: 基于Color Glass Condensate有效理论，采用带跑动耦合（含/不含运动学约束）的领头阶BK方程演化偶极振幅，对HERA的DIS与RHIC/LHC的SIHP数据进行全局联合拟合；引入每台对撞机独立的常数K因子吸收高阶辐射与碎裂效应；使用Hessian方法进行误差分析，并用蒙特卡洛贝叶斯推断验证。

Result: 全局χ²/d.o.f.接近1；RHIC的K因子约为LHC的两倍，与阈值重求和一环计算一致；SIHP数据显著收紧偶极振幅演化速度约束，且不破坏DIS拟合；碎片函数选择和因子化尺度是K因子的主要系统不确定性来源。

Conclusion: DIS与SIHP数据在BK框架下具有互补性，支持BK演化作为小x强子结构的统一描述；需在未来精度分析中重点改进碎片函数和因子化尺度处理以降低系统误差。

Abstract: We present the first simultaneous fit to deep inelastic scattering (DIS) reduced cross sections from HERA and forward single inclusive hadron production (SIHP) from RHIC and the LHC in which the dipole amplitude is obtained from Balitsky--Kovchegov (BK) evolution within the Color Glass Condensate effective theory. We demonstrate that, using the LO BK equation with running coupling (with or without kinematical constraint) and a constant per-collider $K$-factor accounting for higher-order corrections, a global $χ^2/\mathrm{d.o.f.}$ close to unity is achieved in both schemes. The required $K$-factors are about a factor of two larger at RHIC than at the LHC, remarkably in agreement with threshold-resummed one-loop studies of forward production. In our setup and with the data available, we find the two observables to be complementary: the SIHP data tighten the constraint on the evolution speed of the dipole amplitude without introducing tension with the DIS description. We further quantify the dependence of the $K$-factors on the fragmentation-function set and the factorization scale, identifying these as the dominant systematic uncertainties to be addressed in future precision analyses of forward hadron production. We perform a full uncertainty analysis using the Hessian method, validated against a Monte Carlo Bayesian inference study.

</details>


### [143] [Probing collective behaviour of Heavy Quarks through $p_T$-differential radial flow $v_0(p_T)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23499)
*Salvatore Plumari,Maria Lucia Sambataro,Santosh K. Das,Vincenzo Greco*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出重味介子的p_T微分径向流v_0(p_T)作为探测重夸克与夸克胶子等离子体相互作用强度的新观测量，并通过Langevin动力学结合相对论Boltzmann输运方法进行事件逐个分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索重夸克与夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）相互作用强度的新观测量，以更深入理解QGP性质。

Method: 采用Langevin动力学耦合相对论Boltzmann输运方法，在事件逐个基础上研究重味强子的p_T微分径向流v_0(p_T)。

Result: v_0(p_T)在中等p_T区域对重夸克输运系数具有强敏感性；在低p_T区域，该观测量还对强子化过程敏感，且Λ_c重子的v_0(p_T)大于D介子。

Conclusion: 重味强子的v_0(p_T)是一个有效探针，可用于约束重夸克在QGP中的输运性质及强子化机制。

Abstract: We discuss the $p_T$-differential radial flow $v_0(p_T)$ of charmed hadrons within a Langevin dynamics coupled to relativistic Boltzmann transport approach in an event-by-event basis. We propose heavy flavour $v_0(p_T)$ as a novel observable to probe the strength of the interaction of heavy quarks with the expanding Quark-Gluon Plasma. By comparing different temperature dependence for the spatial diffusion coefficient $D_s(T)$ we show that the $v_0(p_T)$ keep a strong sensitivity to the heavy-quark transport coefficients at intermediate $p_T$. At low $p_T$, the observable is also sensitive to hadronization where we observe a larger $v_0(p_T)$ for $Λ_c$ baryons than for $D$ mesons.

</details>


### [144] [Neural Control Variates at LO and NLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23591)
*Theo Heimel,Tilman Plehn,Rebecca Revelli,Sophia Vent,Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经控制变分的相空间积分与事件生成方法，利用两个标准化流构建带符号控制变分，结合神经重要性采样，显著降低LO/NLO预测的计算成本；NLO情形下，该条件神经控制变分可视为可训练的减法项，增强传统物理减法方案的采样性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少相空间积分和事件生成中事件权重的范围，并避免负权重问题，从而提升LO和NLO精度预测的计算效率。

Method: 采用神经控制变分（由两个标准化流构成的带符号控制变分），结合神经重要性采样；在NLO情形中，引入条件神经控制变分作为可训练的减法项。

Result: 显著降低了LO和NLO预测的计算成本，提升了采样性能，尤其在NLO中能有效补充传统物理减法方案。

Conclusion: 神经控制变分为高精度粒子物理模拟提供了一种高效、可训练的减法与重要性采样协同框架，兼具理论严谨性与计算实用性。

Abstract: We employ neural control variates to minimize the range of event weights and avoid negative weights for phase-space integration and event generation. A signed control variate, built from two normalizing flows, fulfills both tasks. Combined with neural importance sampling, it significantly reduces the computational cost of LO and NLO predictions. For the NLO case, our conditional neural control variate can be viewed as a trainable subtraction term, complementing the established physics subtraction schemes for enhanced sampling performance.

</details>


### [145] [Mass and Decay Properties of Toponium Using SUSY QM Factorization Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23643)
*Bhavishya Tepan,Manan Shah,P C Vinodkumar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文应用超对称量子力学(SUSY QM)因子化方法研究顶夸克偶素(t̄t)的Cornell势，成功计算了其质量、束缚能及自旋相关的超精细劈裂，并分析了赝标量(ηt)和矢量(Θt)态的衰变性质与空间特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究顶夸克偶素这一最重的夸克偶素系统，利用SUSY QM方法精确计算其能谱、衰变特性及空间结构。

Method: 采用超对称量子力学(SUSY QM)因子化方法处理Cornell势，结合波函数分析计算质量、束缚能、超精细劈裂、衰变宽度及空间半径等物理量。

Result: 得到ηt和Θt的质量分别为344.114 GeV和344.141 GeV；ηt→gg是主导衰变道，宽度为2.57 MeV；平均半径、均方根半径和最概然半径均在0.015–0.025 fm范围内。

Conclusion: SUSY QM方法适用于顶夸克偶素的精确研究，结果揭示其强束缚、短程特性及显著的胶子主导衰变行为。

Abstract: The Supersymmetric quantum mechanics (SUSY QM) factorization method is applied to study the Cornell potential in the context of toponium $(t\bar{t})$, the heaviest quarkonium system. The method is successfully applied to determine the mass, binding energy, and spin-dependent hyperfine splitting, giving pseudoscalar $(η_t)$ and vector $(Θ_t)$ state masses of 344.114 GeV and 344.141 GeV, respectively. The decay properties of both the pseudoscalar and vector states are also investigated through their respective decay widths, highlighting the crucial role of the bound-state wavefunction. Among the decay channels, the di-gluonic $(η_t\rightarrow gg)$ mode is found to be dominant, with an estimated decay width of 2.57 MeV. In addition, the spatial characteristics of toponium are examined by evaluating the mean radius, root-mean-square (RMS) radius, and most probable radius, all of which are found to lie within the range of $0.015\text{--}0.025\,\mathrm{fm}$.

</details>


### [146] [T-odd transverse momentum dependent gluon distributions for tensor polarized deuteron in a spectator model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23692)
*Xiupeng Xie,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于计算张量极化氘核内T-奇横向动量依赖分布函数（TMDs）的模型，基于氘核发射类时虚胶子的假设，并通过单胶子交换引入终态相互作用以产生非零T-奇效应，推导出六个T-奇胶子TMDs的解析表达式并给出其对纵向动量分数x和横动量k_T的依赖数值结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究张量极化氘核中胶子的T-奇横向动量依赖分布函数（TMDs），以深入理解强子结构中的自旋-动量关联及时间反演破坏效应。

Method: 采用氘核发射类时虚胶子的模型，残余系统视为单粒子旁观者，其质量由谱函数描述；通过单胶子交换引入终态相互作用，从而生成非零T-奇TMDs；推导六个T-奇胶子TMDs的解析表达式。

Result: 得到了六个T-奇胶子TMDs的解析形式，并给出了它们随纵向动量分数x和横动量k_T变化的数值结果。

Conclusion: 该模型为张量极化氘核中胶子TMDs提供了可行的理论框架，揭示了终态相互作用在生成T-奇效应中的关键作用，并为未来实验测量提供了理论预期。

Abstract: We present a model calculation of the T-odd transverse momentum dependent distribution functions (TMDs) for gluons inside a tensor polarized deuteron. The model is built on the assumption that an on-shell deuteron can emit a time-like off-shell gluon, with the residual system treated as a single on-shell spectator particle. The spectator mass is described via a spectral function, which allows it to take real values over a continuous range. The final-state interaction required to generate nonvanishing T-odd functions are implemented via single-gluon exchange between the spectator and the outgoing parton. We derive analytical expressions for six T-odd gluon TMDs, and present numerical results characterizing their dependence on the longitudinal momentum fraction $x$ and the transverse momentum $\bm{k}_T$.

</details>


### [147] [Radiative decays $J/ψ,\,ψ(2S)\rightarrowγη^{(\prime)}$ in perturbative QCD with relativistic corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23737)
*Jun-Kang He,Chao-Jie Fan,Cong Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在微扰QCD框架下计算了J/ψ和ψ(2S)到γη^(′)的辐射衰变，包含所有三类短程贡献（qq̄、双胶子、QED）至q²阶相对论修正；结果对轻锥分布振幅和轻夸克质量鲁棒，但ψ(2S)通道与实验存在显著偏差，提示需引入非微扰机制（如η_c混合）来解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决J/ψ和ψ(2S)辐射衰变到γη和γη'的理论预测与实验数据之间的长期偏差问题，特别是ψ(2S)通道的异常低实验值，探索是否需超出标准微扰QCD机制的新物理或非微扰效应。

Method: 在微扰QCD框架下，系统计入qq̄、双胶子及QED三类短距振幅的q²阶相对论修正；采用两种典型η–η'混合方案分析分支比比值R₁ₛ对混合角的依赖；进一步引入η_c–η/η'混合机制作为可能的非微扰修正并评估其干涉效应。

Result: J/ψ分支比因q²修正增强约两倍，更接近实验；ψ(2S)的q²修正更大且低阶展开收敛差；R₁ₛ对混合角高度敏感，偏好较小混合角；ψ(2S)→γη(′)的LO预测远高于实验，尤其γη通道；引入η_c混合后，干涉效应可使ψ(2S)预测与数据一致，但受混合参数强敏感性限制。

Conclusion: 标准微扰QCD虽对J/ψ衰变给出稳健预测，但无法解释ψ(2S)→γη(′)的实验偏低现象，暗示存在重要非微扰贡献（如η_c混合）；该机制虽能调和理论与实验，但其定量提取受限于混合参数不确定性，需进一步约束。

Abstract: We present the first calculation of the radiative decays $J/ψ,ψ(2S)\rightarrowγη^{(\prime)}$ in perturbative QCD that includes the order-$q^{2}$ relativistic corrections in all three short-distance contributions, namely the quark-antiquark, two-gluon, and QED contributions. The amplitudes are found to be remarkably insensitive to the light-cone distribution amplitude and to the light-quark mass, a robustness that persists through order $q^{2}$ and makes the predictions correspondingly reliable. The relativistic correction enhances the $J/ψ$ branching ratios by roughly a factor of two, narrowing their shortfall from experiment, whereas for the $ψ(2S)$ it is about twice as large as for the $J/ψ$ and the low-order expansion converges poorly. In two representative $η$--$η'$ mixing schemes, the ratio $\mathcal{R}_{1S}=\mathcal{B}(γη')/\mathcal{B}(γη)$ proves sharply sensitive to the mixing angle and favours the smaller of the two. The predicted $ψ(2S)$ rates lie well above the data in both channels, already at leading order, and most severely for the anomalously small $γη$ channel. Such a discrepancy suggests that a mechanism beyond the hard perturbative process is at work. As a physically motivated attempt, we explore the $η_{c}$-mixing contribution, which adds coherently to the perturbative one and is comparable to it in the $γη$ channel, and find that the interference can bring the $ψ(2S)$ rates into agreement with the data, although its extraction is limited by a strong sensitivity to the mixing parameters.

</details>


### [148] [The Higgs boson decay $h \rightarrow bs$ in the NB-LSSM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23862)
*Cai Guo,Xing-Xing Dong,Zhan Cao,Song Gao,Shu-Min Zhao,Tai-Fu Feng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在NB-LSSM模型框架下研究了B介子辐射衰变和希格斯味破坏衰变h→bs，发现h→bs分支比可显著偏离标准模型预期，为探索超出标准模型的新物理提供了理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型（SM）的新物理，特别是通过味物理可观测量来检验新物理模型。

Method: 在下一最小B-L超对称模型（NB-LSSM）框架下，结合B介子辐射衰变过程\bar B→X_sγ的实验约束，研究希格斯粒子味破坏衰变h→bs。

Result: h→bs的分支比可显著偏离标准模型预期，其偏差程度依赖于NB-LSSM中新引入的参数。

Conclusion: NB-LSSM模型中的新物理参数能显著调制希格斯味破坏衰变，表明味可观测量是探测新物理的重要探针。

Abstract: Within the framework of the next to minimum B-L supersymmetric model (NB-LSSM), we investigate the flavor transition process $\bar B\rightarrow X_sγ$. Building upon this foundation, we further discuss the Higgs decay process $h \to bs$ under the constraint from the $\bar B\rightarrow X_sγ$ process. Our study reveals that the branching ratio of $h \to bs$ can significantly deviate from the Standard Model (SM) expectation, depending on the values of the new parameters introduced in the model. This finding highlights the modulation of new physics parameters on the Higgs flavor-violating decay and provides important theoretical grounds for exploring new physics beyond the SM through flavor observables.

</details>


### [149] [Effects of Axion Interactions on Quark Stars in 4D Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23948)
*Rui Zhou,Ming-zheng-xuan Wu,Xin-ran Yang,Chong-long Xie,Min Zhou,Zhi-yang Liu,Shi-jun Mao,Guo-yun Shao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文结合微观轴子扩展的Polyakov-Nambu-Jona-Lasinio模型与四维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力理论，研究了夸克星性质，发现轴子相互作用使状态方程变硬、声速和最大质量提高，而4D EGB修正减弱引力压缩、增大半径与最大质量、降低致密性与表面红移，整体结果更符合观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索夸克星性质，尤其是如何在理论框架中同时纳入新粒子（轴子）效应与高阶引力修正（4D EGB），以解释大质量致密天体的观测特征。

Method: 将微观的轴子扩展PNJL模型与宏观的四维Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力理论耦合，求解星体结构方程（TOV类方程），计算质量-半径关系、声速、致密性、红移等可观测量。

Result: 轴子增强使状态方程变硬，提升最大质量与声速；4D EGB修正削弱引力压缩，增大半径、提高最大质量、降低致密性与表面红移；联合效应使理论预测更符合当前脉冲星质量与半径观测约束。

Conclusion: 轴子动力学与4D EGB引力的协同效应可为描述大质量夸克星提供可行且更具观测兼容性的理论框架。

Abstract: We explore the properties of quark stars by combining the microscopic axion-extended Polyakov--Nambu--Jona-Lasinio model with the macroscopic framework of four-dimensional Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (4D EGB) gravity. Our results show that the inclusion of axion-induced interactions stiffens the equation of state of quark matter, thereby increasing the sound speed and the maximum mass of quark stars. The inclusion of the 4D EGB correction effectively weakens gravitational compression and further modifies the stellar structure, allowing for larger radii and higher maximum masses while reducing the compactness and surface gravitational redshift. Notably, the combined effects yield mass-radius sequences that are more compatible with current observational constraints than those obtained under standard general relativity with conventional quark-matter equations of state. These findings suggest that the interplay between axion dynamics and 4D EGB gravity may provide a viable phenomenological framework for describing massive quark stars.

</details>


### [150] [Electroweak Baryogenesis: Advances in Sphaleron Rate Calculations and Implications of Thermal Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24026)
*Yanda Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了电弱重子生成中sphaleron速率计算的最新进展，并介绍了与sphaleron和瞬子相关的重子数破缺、非阿贝尔规范理论的真空结构及其他拓扑场构型，还探讨了一阶相变对对撞机探测和原初黑洞形成的广泛影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解电弱重子生成机制及sphaleron动力学在宇宙早期演化中的作用，同时促进相关概念（如sphaleron）在中文语境下的传播与标准化。

Method: 文献综述与概念性介绍，辅以 pedagogical 阐释和术语建议。

Result: 系统梳理了sphaleron速率计算的研究现状，提出中文译名“an zi”，并拓展讨论了一阶相变的可观测后果。

Conclusion: sphaleron物理是连接粒子物理与宇宙学的关键桥梁，其理论进展对检验电弱相变及探索新物理具有重要意义。

Abstract: This thesis reviews recent advances in calculating the sphaleron rate, with particular emphasis on electroweak baryogenesis. It also provides pedagogical introductions to sphaleron- and instanton-induced baryon-number violation, the vacuum structure of non-Abelian gauge theories, and other topological field configurations, and suggests "an zi" as a possible Chinese term for "sphaleron" (see p. 3). Broader implications of first-order phase transitions for collider searches and primordial black hole formation are also discussed.

</details>


### [151] [Quantum entanglement within quarkonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24068)
*Wenyu Zhang,Yiyu Zhou,Yang Li,Qun Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非微扰轻前哈密顿框架下研究重夸克偶素中夸克-反夸克的纠缠，通过构造夸克子系统的约化密度矩阵并计算冯·诺依曼熵，将纠缠熵与横向动量依赖部分子分布（TMD）联系起来，并利用BLFQ波函数对粲偶素和底偶素进行数值计算，揭示其对矢量介子极化的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立量子信息测度（如纠缠熵）与强子非微扰结构及部分子可观测量之间的直接联系，探索纠缠熵作为重夸克偶素内部结构新探针的可行性。

Method: 在非微扰轻前哈密顿框架下，对反夸克自由度求迹得到夸克子系统约化密度矩阵，计算冯·诺依曼熵；将熵与自旋0和自旋1夸克偶素的（极化/张量极化）TMD关联；采用基组轻前量子化（BLFQ）获得的轻前波函数进行数值计算，并通过动量空间熵与谐振子表象匹配来确定红外参数。

Result: 发现自旋0偶素的纠缠熵等价于非极化TMD的香农熵（含常数色、自旋修正）；自旋1偶素熵显含极化依赖，与极化及张量极化TMD直接相关；数值结果表明粲偶素与底偶素的纠缠熵对矢量介子极化高度敏感；成功通过红外参数匹配实现熵的物理标定。

Conclusion: 纠缠熵可作为探测重夸克偶素非微扰结构的有效新工具，首次建立了量子纠缠测度与TMD等基本部分子分布之间的严格理论联系。

Abstract: We investigate quark-antiquark entanglement in heavy quarkonium within a nonperturbative light-front Hamiltonian framework. By tracing over the antiquark degrees of freedom in the hadronic state vector, we construct the reduced density matrix of the quark subsystem and compute the associated von Neumann entropy. For spin-0 quarkonia, we show that this entropy reduces to the Shannon entropy of the unpolarized transverse momentum dependent parton distribution (TMD), up to constant color and spin contributions. For spin-1 quarkonia, we derive the explicit polarization dependence of the entropy and connect it to polarized and tensor-polarized TMDs. Using light-front wave functions obtained via basis light-front quantization (BLFQ), we evaluate the entanglement entropy for charmonium and bottomonium states, revealing a pronounced sensitivity to the polarization of vector mesons. Furthermore, we resolve the infrared parameter by matching the momentum-space entropy to a harmonic-oscillator representation. Ultimately, these results establish entanglement entropy as a novel probe of nonperturbative quarkonium structure, forging a direct link between quantum information measures and partonic observables.

</details>


### [152] [Prospects for observing the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ decay at the HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24203)
*Yueling Yang,Bingbing Yang,Junfeng Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了伪标量顶夸克偶素η_t到W+W−的衰变过程，估算了其分支比，并提出在HL-LHC上通过干净的双轻子末态（无b喷注）探测η_t的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受LHC上新近观测到伪标量顶夸克偶素η_t及对其性质持续关注的驱动，本文聚焦于η_t → W+W−这一特征衰变过程。

Method: 基于两种不同η_t衰变常数f_ηt的假设，理论估算η_t → W+W−的分支比；结合HL-LHC预期的η_t事例数（>3×10⁷）和实际轻子识别效率，模拟双轻子末态（ℓ⁻ν̄ℓ ℓ′⁺νℓ′，ℓ,ℓ′=e,μ）的可观测事例数。

Result: 预测在HL-LHC上可观察到数十个来自η_t → W+W− → ℓ⁻ν̄ℓ ℓ′⁺νℓ′的双轻子事例；该通道无b喷注、背景低、信号干净，具备互补性与更高灵敏度。

Conclusion: η_t → W+W− → ℓ⁻ν̄ℓ ℓ′⁺νℓ′是探测η_t的一个有前景的新探针，尤其适合在高亮度LHC上开展实验搜寻。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent observation of the pseudoscalar toponium $η_{t}$ at the LHC and by the ongoing interest in its properties, we phenomenologically investigate the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ process, one of the characteristic decays of toponium $η_{t}$. The branching ratio for the $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ decay is estimated with two scenarios of the decay constant $f_{η_{t}}$. It is found that tens of dilepton events from the cascade decays, $η_{t}$ ${\to}$ $W^{+}W^{-}$ ${\to}$ ${\ell}^{-}\barν_{\ell}{\ell}^{{\prime}+}ν_{{\ell}^{\prime}}$ with ${\ell}$, ${\ell}^{\prime}$ $=$ $e$ and $μ$, could be experimentally observed, when considering the realistic identification efficiency of the charged lepton and assuming more than $3{\times}10^{7}$ $η_{t}$ events available at the future HL-LHC.
